YAMZ DIESEL ENGINES

DIZEL KOMPLEKT BEL SERVIS - official dealer and leading supplier of products from the Yaroslavl Motor Plant (YaMZ, part of the GAZ Group) in Uzbekistan. We offer a full range of solutions for your equipment:

  • YaMZ engines(for cars, special equipment, diesel generator sets)
  • YaMZ gearboxes(checkpoint)
  • Original YaMZ spare parts

We guarantee direct deliveries, factory warranty and the availability of a wide range of products in a warehouse in Tashkent.

Advantages of YaMZ products:

Reliability and availability: The products of the Yaroslavl Motor Plant have been proving their effectiveness in the most difficult operating conditions for decades.

This is the optimal choice for enterprises in Uzbekistan due to its combination of high reliability, durability and ease of maintenance.

  • High resource:YaMZ engines and gearboxes are designed for intensive work and have a long service life.
  • Maintainability:A simple and proven design provides easy access to components and assemblies, minimizing equipment downtime.
  • Versatility:YaMZ products are widely combined with MAZ, Ural, KrAZ, ZIL equipment, as well as with PAZ, LiAZ buses and various types of special equipment.
  • Spare parts availability:An extensive warehouse stock of original components allows us to quickly resolve any maintenance and repair issues.

YaMZ engines for transport, special equipment and diesel generator sets

We supply the entire current model range of YaMZ engines, including V-shaped and modern in-line series:

Gearboxes (Gearboxes) YaMZ

We offer a wide selection of YaMZ manual transmissions, characterized by high reliability and ease of maintenance.

Original spare parts YaMZ in Tashkent

Our warehouse in Tashkent offers the most complete range of original spare parts for the entire YaMZ model range. We guarantee the authenticity of every detail.

 

Contacts: Buy YaMZ products in Uzbekistan

YaMZ is one of the largest Russian enterprises producing multi-purpose diesel engines, clutches, gearboxes, spare parts for them, as well as stationary units based on them. YaMZ produces products exclusively for civilian use.

Yaroslavl engines power more than 300 models of vehicles and special-purpose products produced by enterprises in Russia and Belarus.

YaMZ engines are installed on trucks, long-haul road trains, mining dump trucks, buses, tractors and combine harvesters, road construction equipment, as well as diesel-electric stations.

Our company is an official dealer of PJSC Avtodizel (YaMZ) and supplies original YaMZ engines and spare parts both from stock and on order.

A large number of original YaMZ spare parts (cylinder blocks, crankshafts, liners, pistons, connecting rods, injection pumps, repair kits, etc.) are available at the warehouse in Tashkent. Call us and we will select the necessary spare parts, gearboxes and engines for you.

 

 

You can get detailed information about the benefits of using original YaMZ products, the risks and threats of using alternative products of poor quality, and also check the originality of YaMZ products on the manufacturer’s official website www.ymzmotor.ru in the “Protection against counterfeiting” section by clicking on the YaMZ/YAZDA link. Anti-counterfeit protection

At the warehouse in Tashkent (Republic of Uzbekistan) we have assembled engines, a wide range of original spare parts and consumables for engines. Service specialists provide engine repair and maintenance services, including on-site visits. A guarantee is provided for the products supplied and services provided. If you have a need to purchase engines, gearboxes or spare parts from YaMZ (manufactured by PJSC Avtodizel), then you can contact our company and we will help you select the required product.
